It enforces the BREAKING player CSS rules. ([zeffyr-000/zeffyrmusic](https://github.com/zeffyr-000/zeffyrmusic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/player-debug.md))
- **player-engineer**: Builds the floating Electron companion, YouTube IFrame integration, playback controls, progress reporting, and responsive UI. ([HershelT/mdy-daf-companion](https://github.com/HershelT/mdy-daf-companion/blob/HEAD/mdy-daf-companion/agents/player-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **player-eval**: Screenshot evaluation agent. Analyzes one screenshot and outputs structured JSON perception/decision. ([houseworthe/claude-royale](https://github.com/houseworthe/claude-royale/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/player-eval.md) · ★27)
- **player-facing-ballistics**: A third-person camera can orbit past the soldier's shoulders without rotating the body. A possessed soldier's ballistic origin must **never** move behind the body just because the camera crosshair is pointing behind it. ([Teethree89/grasstex](https://github.com/Teethree89/grasstex/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/player-facing-ballistics.md))
- **player-facing-label-reviewer**: Checks that text a player actually sees never comes from str(model) / a bare __str__ call. Use when a diff builds a broadcast line, a msg() call, narration, or Interaction content that names a character, participant, or opponent, and when reviewing one. Catches a label that renders as "Sheet for X". ([Arx-Game/arxii](https://github.com/Arx-Game/arxii/blob/HEAD/tools/agents/player-facing-label-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **player-mode-feedback**: Implementation: `battle/camera-controls.js` (player possession/HUD/haptic input), `battle/modules/41-squad-status-overlay.js` (offscreen tactical unit cues). This is a presentation layer plus a **player-only sprint budget**; combat hits, casualties, wounds, shooting, and AI orders remain with their existing owners. ([Teethree89/grasstex](https://github.com/Teethree89/grasstex/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/player-mode-feedback.md))
- **player-psychologist**: Applies player psychology principles to game design - motivation, engagement, flow states, and player types. Use when designing for retention, understanding player behavior, or creating compelling experiences. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/game-design/agents/player-psychologist.md) · ★19)
- **player-psychology-guide**: Shape player motivation, emotional journey, and engagement. Use this agent when designing difficulty curves, reward systems, emotional beats, or diagnosing why players quit or disengage. Synthesizes wisdom from Miyamoto (challenge and accomplishment), Pardo (easy to learn, impossible to master), Rigopulos (accessibility bridges), and Jaffe (real emotions). &lt;example&gt; Context: Players are quitting at a specific point user: "Analytics show 60% of players quit at level 5. The difficulty seems fair to us." assistant: "I'll consult the player-psychology-guide to diagnose the motivation breakdown."… ([manifoldlogic/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/manifoldlogic/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/game-design/agents/player-psychology-guide.md))
- **player-researcher**: Designs player surveys, interviews, and behavioral analysis. Use when collecting player opinions at scale, segmenting audiences, or understanding player preferences. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/user-research/agents/player-researcher.md) · ★19)
- **player-researcher**: Research source material for players. No spoilers or secrets. ([protoLabsAI/mythx-mcp](https://github.com/protoLabsAI/mythx-mcp/blob/HEAD/world-builder/agents/player-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **player-researcher**: researches the target player. invoke when any new feature or strategic decision. ([Imdevsup/game_builder_claude](https://github.com/Imdevsup/game_builder_claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/player-researcher.md))
- **player-runner**: Builds a Windows player from a named build profile (or takes an existing build), runs the requested -mc-run sessions in it with timeouts, quotes the requested report sections verbatim, and restores the Editor's build state. Spawn ONLY for a build or player run the user authorized this session. ([A-Van-Gestel/Unity-Minecraft_Clone](https://github.com/A-Van-Gestel/Unity-Minecraft_Clone/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/player-runner.md))
- **player-segmentation-expert**: Designs player segmentation strategies for targeted content and offers. Use when personalizing experiences, targeting offers, or analyzing player cohorts. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/operations/agents/player-segmentation-expert.md) · ★19)
- **player-simulator**: Plays builds as defined personas and reports experience and friction honestly. Use for persona playtests, first-session and FTUE checks, confusion and drop-off points, softlock and difficulty probes through the playtest runner and engine MCP bridges, and evidence for the playthrough and critic reviews. ([samsamurai301/arcosian](https://github.com/samsamurai301/arcosian/blob/HEAD/plugins/arcosian/agents/player-simulator.md))
- **player-writable-endpoint-reviewer**: Checks whether a REST route lets a player create, update or delete rows they should only gain through a service (a grant, a ritual, a purchase, a check). Use when a diff adds or changes a ViewSet, a router registration, a permission class, a serializer used for writes, or http_method_names, and when reviewing one. Catches the ModelViewSet whose router writes any signed-in player can call, so a player gives themselves a gift or refills their anima with one POST. ([Arx-Game/arxii](https://github.com/Arx-Game/arxii/blob/HEAD/tools/agents/player-writable-endpoint-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **player_management_ms**: You are a subagent assigned exclusively to `./services/player_management_ms`. You read, explore, and edit files only within that directory. You must never touch any file outside of it. ([AntonioCacciotti/llm_orchestrator](https://github.com/AntonioCacciotti/llm_orchestrator/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/player_management_ms.md) · ★1)
- **playfab-helper**: PlayFab REST API specialist that assists with adding, modifying, and testing PlayFab API tool definitions in the Rust CLI. &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to add a new PlayFab API endpoint. user: "Add the GetPlayerProfile API to the player module" assistant: "I'll use the playfab-helper agent to look up the API spec and scaffold the implementation." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User needs to verify API parameter correctness. user: "Check if the SearchItems parameters match the official docs" assistant: "I'll use the playfab-helper agent to cross-reference the official PlayFab REST API document… ([akiojin/playfab-cli](https://github.com/akiojin/playfab-cli/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playfab-helper.md))
- **playground**: Build and test a customized ADK Bidi-streaming application in src/playgrounds directory based on user request ([arakitakashi/homework-coach-robo](https://github.com/arakitakashi/homework-coach-robo/blob/HEAD/ref/adk-streaming-guide-main/.claude/agents/playground.md) · ★1)
- **playground-driver**: One-shot batch executor for playground e2e scenario runs. Drives the containerized AUT, judges, writes report.md + results.json. Dispatched headlessly by the playground-e2e make rules; not for interactive debugging (use the /playground-driver skill for that). ([gnoverse/gno-mcp](https://github.com/gnoverse/gno-mcp/blob/HEAD/playground/.claude/agents/playground-driver.md) · ★3)
- **playground-hosting**: The browser playground (`cmd/playground/`) is built by `scripts/build-playground.sh &lt;output&gt;/playground/&lt;version&gt; &lt;version&gt;` and served at `https://haproxy-haptic.org/playground/&lt;version&gt;/`. Releases use the release version and are immutable; `main` publishes the moving `dev` version. Publication, version retention, and the Brotli transport are described in [GitHub Pages packaging](github-pages.md ([HAProxy-Template-IC/haproxy-template-ingress-controller](https://github.com/HAProxy-Template-IC/haproxy-template-ingress-controller/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/playground-hosting.md) · 2 copies)
- **playground-qa**: Runs docs/playground.md of Payment Gateway for Redsys & WooCommerce Lite literally, with fresh context. Use at sprint closes and at the Phase 7 gate. ([joseconti/woo-redsys-gateway-light](https://github.com/joseconti/woo-redsys-gateway-light/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playground-qa.md) · ★4)
- **playground-reviewer**: Reviews TypeScript/React playground code for component quality, proper typing, performance, accessibility, and docmux conventions. Use after modifying playground components. ([aguschirico/docmux](https://github.com/aguschirico/docmux/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playground-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **playground-testing**: - The playground (`apps/playground`) is a showcase and E2E testing ground for Rozenite DevTools plugins. Every screen exists to trigger one plugin behavior; DevTools is where you observe the result. - Follow `docs/agents/e2e-testing.md` to reach the app, confirm Rozenite is installed on the simulator, and open React Native DevTools. - DevTools UI is a normal web page — agents can read and interact ([callstackincubator/rozenite](https://github.com/callstackincubator/rozenite/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/playground-testing.md) · ★678)
- **playground-verifier**: Boots the plugin in an ephemeral WordPress Playground instance and verifies it actually runs — activation, REST routes, admin screens, deactivation, uninstall. Reports findings; never modifies the working tree. Invoke before opening a feature PR and before tagging a release, alongside security-reviewer. ([pluginslab/wp-agentic-kit](https://github.com/pluginslab/wp-agentic-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playground-verifier.md) · ★20)
- **PlaylistMiner .NET Expert**: Expert .NET 10 software engineer for PlaylistMiner backend development. Follows Clean Architecture, TDD, and SOLID principles. ([ManikantaR/playlistminer](https://github.com/ManikantaR/playlistminer/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/expert-dotnet-engineer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **PlaylistMiner Frontend Expert**: Expert Next.js 14 / TypeScript frontend engineer for PlaylistMiner. Follows component-driven TDD with Jest and Playwright. ([ManikantaR/playlistminer](https://github.com/ManikantaR/playlistminer/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/frontend-engineer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **playmode-tester**: Use after implementation to verify Unity Play Mode behavior with data assertions, NUnit, or playtest DSL. Do not use for scene authoring, code changes, or screenshot-only acceptance. ([german-krasnikov/unity-biome-mcp](https://github.com/german-krasnikov/unity-biome-mcp/blob/HEAD/unity-plugin/ClientSkills/agents/playmode-tester.md) · ★33)
- **playtest**: Extended playtest session — plays the game as a real player and documents feedback. Use for longer dedicated playtesting sessions (e.g., final check after a feature is complete), NOT for quick spot-checks during development (just run snapshot mode directly for those). ([emernic/pandemic-cli](https://github.com/emernic/pandemic-cli/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-agent**: Runs deterministic browser playtests for the mascot across desktop, mobile, touch, resize, obstacles, route navigation, hidden-tab behaviour, and reduced motion. ([shantanusoam/portfolio-main](https://github.com/shantanusoam/portfolio-main/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-agent.md))
- **playtest-analyst**: Synthesizes playtest observations, findings, and priority recommendations. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-analyst.md) · ★110)
- **playtest-analyst**: Takes the list of problems from a playtest, reads the code and the GDD to find the causes, and organizes them into a work document (docs/playtest/*.md) that another (cheaper) model can implement as written without re-analyzing. Does not change code. Called by the playtest skill — used when the user asks for analysis of a list of problems written down while playing. ([dudwns0921/claude-gamedev-kit](https://github.com/dudwns0921/claude-gamedev-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-analyst.md) · ★11)
- **playtest-analyst**: Analyze playtest reports and bug reports — extract repro steps, identify root cause, propose minimal fix + regression test. Bridges qualitative feedback (player frustration, confusion) with quantitative repro (logs, screenshots, save files). ([Simone-Tarantino/godot-superpowers](https://github.com/Simone-Tarantino/godot-superpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **playtest-analyzer**: Analyze playtest telemetry JSONL and batch calibration harness output to extract win rates, MBTI distribution, bug patterns, and calibration suggestions across encounters ([MasterDD-L34D/Game](https://github.com/MasterDD-L34D/Game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-analyzer.md))
- **playtest-app-architect**: Specifies the playtest app that plays the tabletop rules: host, transport, client shape, and what a session records. Use for the app ADR and for any question about how a human game reaches the engine or the learning pipeline. ([Downfallz/maintest](https://github.com/Downfallz/maintest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-app-architect.md))
- **playtest-auditor**: Independent reviewer for a game project or build before it reaches players. Checks that it runs, that its controls, screens and end states hold up, that assets are original and credited, and that no secrets ship. Reports findings with evidence and does not edit the project. Use after a build is ready, or when the release-check skill asks for a second pass. ([ZenZeiTen/synthapps](https://github.com/ZenZeiTen/synthapps/blob/HEAD/gamedev-forge/plugins/gamedev-forge/agents/playtest-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-bot**: Plays a browser game with Playwright to find bugs, and reports them in playtest/report.md. Use proactively after gameplay or UI changes to a browser game. ([ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins](https://github.com/ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins/blob/HEAD/playtest-bot/agents/playtest-bot.md))
- **playtest-coordinator**: Use this agent when planning or analyzing playtests: session structure, observation or think-aloud protocols, metrics and telemetry schemas, funnel analysis, death heatmaps, A/B tests for balance, or recruiting players without bias. It turns player observations into findings you can act on. ([HermeticOrmus/claude-code-game-development](https://github.com/HermeticOrmus/claude-code-game-development/blob/HEAD/plugins/playtesting/agents/playtest-coordinator.md) · ★81)
- **playtest-coordinator**: Plans and runs playtesting sessions and feedback collection. Use when organizing playtests, designing feedback collection, or analyzing playtest results. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/user-research/agents/playtest-coordinator.md) · ★19)
- **playtest-coordinator**: **Descrizione**: [EVOLVED via tournament 2026-04-25 - 4 traits inherited] Gestisce la creazione e valutazione di demo giocabili per meccaniche emergenti ([MasterDD-L34D/Game](https://github.com/MasterDD-L34D/Game/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-coordinator.md))
- **playtest-critic**: A fresh-eyes playtester. Use after a feature ships or when something feels off but nobody can say why. Give it screenshots, a run description, or a feature summary and it reports what a first-time player would be confused by, bored by, or never discover, ranked by how likely it is to make them quit. ([tiennguyen03/infected-coop-shooter](https://github.com/tiennguyen03/infected-coop-shooter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-critic.md))
- **playtest-lead** ([odysseyalive/claude-workforce](https://github.com/odysseyalive/claude-workforce/blob/HEAD/fixtures/scripts/orgsync-charted-elsewhere/.claude/agents/playtest-lead.md) · ★2)
- **playtest-lead**: Plans and runs playtesting — the empirical "is it fun / clear / fair?" gate of game development — and turns observations into actionable design/feel feedback. Use when someone needs a playtest planned, playtest feedback analyzed, a feature's fun/difficulty/clarity evaluated, telemetry/metrics designed, or a go/no-go on whether the core loop is working. The games analog of the qa-test-architect: fun is verified by real play, not asserted. ([vezril/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/vezril/claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-lead.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-ops-sme**: You answer questions about the **playtest-ops** domain, using only the `playtest-ops` namespace. ([nickejanssen/arcwright](https://github.com/nickejanssen/arcwright/blob/HEAD/team-ai/agents/playtest-ops-sme.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-ops-sme**: Playtest preparation, harness operation, research, and publishing. Answers design and decision questions from the playtest-ops docs, with citations. ([nickejanssen/arcwright](https://github.com/nickejanssen/arcwright/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/team-ai-playtest-ops-sme.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-planner**: Builds a manual play test plan from the current branch's changes - concrete in-game steps for Gothic 1 and Gothic 2 (and mods if relevant), required DeveloperConfig flags, expected results, and which log tags to watch. Use before a playtest session, a PR, or a release build. ([Gothic-Unity-Project/Gothic-Unity](https://github.com/Gothic-Unity-Project/Gothic-Unity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-planner.md) · ★33)
- **playtest-qa**: Runs the UI in a browser (Playwright) with demo data and reports what a player actually sees: screenshots, console errors, reduced-motion, mobile viewport, frame-rate sample, and whether game pages look different from normal mode. Use after any game-mode UI change and before a PR. Read-only against the app. ([frmingest/Aladdin](https://github.com/frmingest/Aladdin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-qa**: 바뀐 것에 맞는 봇·점검 스크립트를 돌리고 스크린숏을 사람 눈으로 본 뒤, 기준 수치와 견줘 판정을 보고한다. 마일스톤을 끝낼 때, 손맛·화면·흐름이 바뀌었을 때 쓴다. 코드는 고치지 않는다. ([sksskdf/mythic-relics](https://github.com/sksskdf/mythic-relics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-reviewer**: The independent playtest reviewer for a run: builds and plays the game itself through the scripted-input seam, captures its own frame sequences and logs, and scores all six rubric dimensions from what it actually observed. It is never the implementer and is read-only on source. Invoke once per review round with a small handoff (project root, engine binary, build entry, input seam, acceptance criteria, round number). ([HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin](https://github.com/HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **playtest-runner**: Runs a long Easy Red 2 play-test unattended — deploy, launch, drive the game, wait on log evidence, and report what the decision trace actually shows.
